function removeVoxel(x,y,z){ world.delete(voxelKey(x,y,z)); }
function hasVoxel(x,y,z){ return world.has(voxelKey(x,y,z)); }

PRNG:
```
function mulberry32(a) {
  return function() {
    a |= 0; a = a + 0x6D2B79F5 | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ a >>> 15, 1 | a);
    t = t + Math.imul(t ^ t >>> 7, 61 | t) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ t >>> 14) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
```
Standard. Need use `Math.imul`.

`formatTime(hours)`:
```
function formatTime(h) {
  let totalMinutes = Math.floor(((h % 24) + 24) % 24 * 60 + 1e-6);
  let hh = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24;
  let mm = totalMinutes % 60;
  return String(hh).padStart(2,'0') + ':' + String(mm).padStart(2,'0');
}
```
Need handle 24 -> 00:00? Slider 0-24. If hours=24, should show 00:00 or 24:00? Clock HH:MM likely 00:00. Use modulo. At 24, 24%24=0 => 00:00. Fine. At 10.5 => 10:30.

`skyColorAt(h)`:
```
const SKY_KEYS = [
 { h:0, c:0x0a0f24 }, ...
];
const SKY_COLORS = SKY_KEYS.map(k => new THREE.Color(k.c));
const tempColorA = new THREE.Color(), tempColorB = new THREE.Color();
function skyColorAt(h) {
  let hh = ((h % 24) + 24) % 24;
  // keys include 0 and 24; if hh < 0? Use hh. Need segment.
  for (let i=0;i<SKY_KEYS.length-1;i++){
    const a=SKY_KEYS[i], b=SKY_KEYS[i+1];
    if (hh >= a.h && hh <= b.h) {
      const t = (hh - a.h)/(b.h - a.h);
      return skyColor.lerpColors(SKY_COLORS[i], SKY_COLORS[i+1], t);
    }
  }
  return skyColor.copy(SKY_COLORS[0]);
}
```
At hh=24, we normalize to 0, so segment 0..4.5 works. Good. But key 24 unused except maybe if h=24? We normalize. Fine. Could use hh = h; if h>=24 set 0. We'll normalize. Good.

```
const now = performance.now();
let dt = (now - lastTimeMs)/1000;
if (!isFinite(dt) || dt < 0) dt = 0;
dt = Math.min(dt, 0.1);

elapsedSeconds += dt;

animateKoi(elapsedSeconds);
updatePetals(dt, elapsedSeconds);

World generation details:

`rebuildWorld()`:
```
function rebuildWorld() {
  // remove old
  removeInstancedMesh(staticMesh); staticMesh=null;
  removeInstancedMesh(waterMesh); waterMesh=null;
  removeInstancedMesh(koiMesh); koiMesh=null;
  world.clear();
  resetLayout();
  const rng = mulberry32(seed);
  const edgePhases = { a: rng()*TAU, b: rng()*TAU, c: rng()*TAU };
  layout.edgePhases = edgePhases;
  const edgeRadius = (theta) => 22 + 1.4*Math.sin(3*theta + edgePhases.a) + 0.9*Math.sin(5*theta + edgePhases.b) + 0.5*Math.sin(8*theta + edgePhases.c);
  layout.edgeRadiusFn = edgeRadius;
  // generate island top/dirt
  for x from -26 to 26, z from -26 to 26:
     const theta = Math.atan2(z, x);
     const r = Math.hypot(x,z);
     const jitter = (rng()*2-1)*0.6;
     let land = r <= edgeRadius(theta) + jitter;
     if (Math.abs(x)<=4 && z>=8 && z<=20) land = true;
     if (land) {
       const grassColors = [0x6aa84f,0x7bbf5a,0x5c9644];
       setVoxel(x,0,z, grassColors[Math.floor(rng()*3)]);
       setVoxel(x,-1,z, rng()<0.5?0x7a5a3c:0x6b4d33);
       setVoxel(x,-2,z, rng()<0.5?0x7a5a3c:0x6b4d33);
     }
  }
```

- Grass shades chosen per cell. Dirt shades. Use rng.

function animateWater(t) {
  if (!waterMesh) return;

    if (waterRestPending) {
      for (let i=0;i<layout.pond.waterCells.length;i++) { const [x,z] = ...; dummy.position.set(x,-0.1,z); dummy.scale.set(1,0.8,1); dummy.rotation.set(0,0,0); dummy.updateMatrix(); waterMesh.setMatrixAt(i,dummy.matrix); }
      waterMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
      waterRestPending = false;
    }
    return;
  }
  const cells = layout.pond.waterCells;
  for i: const x=cells[i][0], z=cells[i][1]; const y = -0.1 + 0.09*Math.sin(t*1.6 + x*0.7 + z*0.5); dummy.position.set(x,y,z); dummy.scale.set(1,0.8,1); dummy.rotation.set(0,0,0); dummy.updateMatrix(); waterMesh.setMatrixAt(i,dummy.matrix);

Petal update details:
```
function updatePetals(dt, t) {
  if (!petalMesh || petalsTarget <= 0) { if (petalMesh) petalMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; return; }
  const windX = WIND_DIR.x, windZ = WIND_DIR.z;
  const gust = 0.65 + 0.35 * Math.sin(t*0.6 + 0.4);
  const driftX = windX * wind * 5.5 * gust;
  const driftZ = windZ * wind * 5.5 * gust;
  for (let i=0;i<petalsTarget;i++) {
    const i3=i*3;
    petalPos[i3+1] -= petalFall[i]*dt;
    const sway = Math.sin(t*1.2 + petalSwayPhase[i]) * petalSwayAmp[i] * 0.6;
    petalPos[i3] += (driftX + sway) * dt; // sway as velocity? Maybe sway oscillation. 
    petalPos[i3+2] += (driftZ + Math.cos(t*1.1 + petalSwayPhase[i]) * petalSwayAmp[i] * 0.4) * dt;
    petalRot[i3] += petalSpin[i3]*dt; ...
    const horiz = Math.hypot(petalPos[i3], petalPos[i3+2]);
    if (petalPos[i3+1] < -26 || horiz > 46) {
      respawnPetal(i);
    }
    dummy.position.set(...);
    dummy.rotation.set(...);
    dummy.scale.set(1,1,1);
    dummy.updateMatrix();
    petalMesh.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
  }
  petalMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
}
```
- Respawn: new position on disc radius 30 at y 34..44, disc shifted upwind by wind*18. Use petalRng? Runtime effects use own mulberry32 stream. We need a persistent petalRng created in rebuild. `petalRng` global let. In respawn, use petalRng. `const ang = petalRng()*TAU; const rad = Math.sqrt(petalRng())*30; x = -WIND_DIR.x * wind * 18 + Math.cos(ang)*rad; z = -WIND_DIR.z * wind * 18 + Math.sin(ang)*rad; y = 34 + petalRng()*10;` Then reset rotations maybe random; fall maybe random? Spec says respawning above island; can keep fall/spin or randomize. We can randomize rotations and maybe fall? Not necessary. Keep fall/spin/sway. Reset rotations to random. Good.
- Need if wind=0, shifted none. Good.
- WindDir constant: `const WIND_DIR = new THREE.Vector3(0.82,0,0.57).normalize();` top. Wind slider changes magnitude. Good.
- Petals initial spread y uniformly -26 to 44. Good.

- Update:
```
function updateFireflies(t) {
  if (!firefliesPoints || !fireflyData.length) return;
  const posAttr = firefliesPoints.geometry.attributes.position;
  const colAttr = firefliesPoints.geometry.attributes.color;
  for i:
    const f = fireflyData[i];
    const x = f.bx + Math.sin(t*f.fx1 + f.px1)*f.ax1 + Math.sin(t*f.fx2 + f.px2)*f.ax2;
    const y = f.by + Math.sin(t*f.fy1 + f.py1)*f.ay1 + Math.sin(t*f.fy2 + f.py2)*f.ay2;
    const z = f.bz + Math.sin(t*f.fz1 + f.pz1)*f.az1 + Math.sin(t*f.fz2 + f.pz2)*f.az2;
    posAttr.setXYZ(i,x,y,z);
    const tw = 0.35 + 0.65 * (0.5 + 0.5*Math.sin(t*f.twinkleFreq + f.twinklePhase));
    colAttr.setXYZ(i, f.cr*tw, f.cg*tw, f.cb*tw);
  posAttr.needsUpdate = true;
  colAttr.needsUpdate = true;
}
```
- Need `fireflyData` store baseColor linear components. Use `new THREE.Color(0xd9ff8a)`; cr=color.r etc. 
- Note PointsMaterial with vertexColors true and `color` attribute. Good. `map` radial gradient. `opacity` nightFactor. `visible` set. 
- If fireflies checkbox off, visible false. Still update? Frame order says fireflies. Could skip update if not visible to save. But spec says update maybe. We can update anyway but cheap 80. Fine.
- Firefly base positions within radius 19, y 1.5..11. Good.

Glow texture:
```
function makeGlowTexture() {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = 64; canvas.height = 64;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  const grad = ctx.createRadialGradient(32,32,0,32,32,32);
  grad.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,255,220,1)');
  grad.addColorStop(0.25, 'rgba(220,255,150,0.9)');
  grad.addColorStop(0.6, 'rgba(180,255,100,0.35)');
  grad.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  ctx.fillStyle = grad;
  ctx.fillRect(0,0,64,64);
  const tex = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; // maybe
  return tex;
}
```
- Need no Math.random. Fine.

- Tiers.
```
const halfWidths=[5,4,3,2,1];
const wallHeights=[4,3,3,3,3];
let y0=3;
for (let i=0;i<5;i++) {
  const hw=halfWidths[i], wh=wallHeights[i];
  for (let row=0; row<wh; row++) {
    const y=y0+row;
    for (let x=-hw;x<=hw;x++) {
      for (let z=-hw;z<=hw;z++) {
        if (Math.abs(x)!==hw && Math.abs(z)!==hw) continue;
        let color;
        if (row===0) color = TIMBER_COLORS[...];
        else if (row===wh-1) color = 0xb5322a;
        else if ((Math.abs(x)===hw && Math.abs(z)===hw) || ((x+z)%2===0)) color = TIMBER_COLORS[...];
        else color = 0xf1ece0;
        setVoxel(x,y,z,color);
      }
    }
  }
  if (i===0) {
    for (let row=0; row<3; row++) {
      const y=y0+row;
      for (let x=-1;x<=1;x++) {
        removeVoxel(x,y,hw);
        setVoxel(x,y,hw-1,0x0c0c0e);
      }
    }
  }
  const yr=y0+wh;
  const eave=hw+3;
  for (let x=-eave;x<=eave;x++) for (let z=-eave;z<=eave;z++) {
    const edge = (Math.abs(x)===eave || Math.abs(z)===eave);
    setVoxel(x,yr,z, edge ? 0x3a3f48 : 0x262a31);
  }
  // flare
  for (let x=-eave;x<=eave;x++) for (let z=-eave;z<=eave;z++) {
    if (Math.abs(x)!==eave && Math.abs(z)!==eave) continue;
    const nearCorner = Math.abs(x) >= eave-1 && Math.abs(z) >= eave-1;
    if (!nearCorner) continue;
    const exactCorner = Math.abs(x)===eave && Math.abs(z)===eave;
    removeVoxel(x,yr,z);
    setVoxel(x,yr + (exactCorner?2:1), z, 0x3a3f48);
  }
  // layer yr+1
  const eave2=hw+1;
  for (let x=-eave2;x<=eave2;x++) for (let z=-eave2;z<=eave2;z++) {
    setVoxel(x,yr+1,z, 0x262a31);
  }
  y0 = yr + 2;
}
```

So flare adds 3 cells per corner? For each corner, cells: (8,8) exact, (8,7), (7,8) near. That's 3 per corner, 12 cells lifted. Good. "cells within 1 of a corner are lifted from yr to yr+1" Could include (7,7)? But that's not on outermost ring, so not. Good.

`computePond(rng)`:
- Need use `layout.edgeRadiusFn`. 
```
let px = 13 + rng();
let pz = -1 + rng()*3;
let rx = 4.8 + rng()*0.6;
let rz = 9 + rng();
const wobbleA = rng()*TAU;
const wobbleB = rng()*TAU;
let cells = new Set();
let cellList = [];
for (let attempt=0; attempt<7; attempt++) {
  cells = new Set(); cellList = [];
  const boundX = Math.ceil(px + rx*1.4);
  const boundZ = Math.ceil(pz + rz*1.4);
  const minX = Math.floor(px - rx*1.4);
  const minZ = Math.floor(pz - rz*1.4);
  for (let x=minX;x<=boundX;x++) {
    if (x < 9) continue;
    for (let z=minZ;z<=boundZ;z++) {
      const theta = Math.atan2(z,x);
      if (Math.hypot(x,z) > layout.edgeRadiusFn(theta) - 3) continue;
      const dx = x - px, dz = z - pz;
      const angle = Math.atan2(dz, dx);
      const wobble = 1 + 0.07*Math.sin(3*angle + wobbleA) + 0.05*Math.sin(5*angle + wobbleB);
      const rxw = rx * wobble;
      const rzw = rz * wobble;
      if ((dx*dx)/(rxw*rxw) + (dz*dz)/(rzw*rzw) <= 1) {
        const key = x + ',' + z;
        cells.add(key);
        cellList.push([x,z]);
      }
    }
  }
  if (cells.size >= 120) break;
  rx *= 1.08;
  rz *= 1.08;
}
layout.pond = { px, pz, rx, rz, cells, cellList, waterCells: [] };
```
- Note if after 7 attempts still <120, rx/rz have been scaled 7 times? Loop attempt 0 initial, then if <120 scale; attempts 1..6. At attempt 6 if still <120, scale again after loop? Actually after attempt 6, if <120, rx*=1.08 then loop ends. So final rx is scaled but cells from attempt 6 not scaled. We should not scale after last attempt. Better loop `for (let attempt=0; attempt<7; attempt++) { ... if (cells.size>=120) break; if (attempt<6) { rx*=1.08; rz*=1.08; } }`. This matches at most 6 retries. Good. Need store final cells. If attempt 6 still <120, keep. Fine.
- Maybe x>=9 but pond center px 13-14, rx scaled huge; x max bound. Edge radius constraint. Good.
- Need `layout.pond.cells = cells` Set of keys, `cellList`. Good.
- Wobble angle uses atan2(dz,dx). Fine.

`buildBridge(rng)`:
- row = Math.round(layout.pond.pz). 
- Find xs:
```
let xs = layout.pond.cellList.filter(c => c[1]===row).map(c=>c[0]);
if (xs.length===0) {
  // choose nearest row present
  const rows = new Set(layout.pond.cellList.map(c=>c[1]));
  let bestRow = row; let bestDist = Infinity;
  for (const r of rows) { const d = Math.abs(r - layout.pond.pz); if (d < bestDist) { bestDist = d; bestRow = r; } }
  row = bestRow;
  xs = layout.pond.cellList.filter(c=>c[1]===row).map(c=>c[0]);
}
if (xs.length===0) { layout.bridge = {z:row,start:0,end:0}; return; }
const west = Math.min(...xs);
const east = Math.max(...xs);
const start = west - 1;
const end = east + 1;
const span = end - start;
const deckYAt = (x) => {
  const t = span > 0 ? (x - start) / span : 0;
  return 1 + Math.round(1.2 * Math.sin(Math.PI * t));
};
for (let x=start; x<=end; x++) {
  const y = deckYAt(x);
  for (let dz=-1; dz<=1; dz++) {
    const color = ((x + dz) % 2 === 0) ? 0x3b2a20 : 0x4a3628; // alternating planks
    setVoxel(x,y,row+dz,color);
  }
}
const mid = Math.round((start+end)/2);
for (const x of [start,end,mid]) {
  const y = deckYAt(x) + 1;
  for (const dz of [-1,1]) setVoxel(x,y,row+dz,0x8a2a20);
}
layout.bridge = {z:row,start,end};
```
- Note if bridge deck overwrites torii? Torii z=18, row pz -1..2, so no. Good.
- Deck at y=1 or 2. Water y=-0.1. Good.
- Planks alternating. Good.

- Need `isTreeSpotValid` function. Could define inside buildTrees or separate. It uses layout. We'll define separate function `isTreeSpotValid(x,z,relaxed)`.
```
function isTreeSpotValid(x,z,relaxed) {
  const distCentre = Math.hypot(x,z);
  if (distCentre < 11 || distCentre > 17.5) return false;
  let azDeg = Math.atan2(x,z) * 180 / Math.PI;
  if (azDeg < 0) azDeg += 360;
  if (azDeg < 130 || azDeg > 340) return false;
  if (!hasVoxel(x,0,z)) return false;
  const pondDist = relaxed ? 2.0 : 3.5;
  for (const [px,pz] of layout.pond.cellList) {
    const dx = x-px, dz = z-pz;
    if (dx*dx + dz*dz < pondDist*pondDist) return false;
  }
  const pathDist = relaxed ? 1.0 : 2.5;
  for (const [px,pz] of layout.pathCellList) {
    const dx = x-px, dz = z-pz;
    if (dx*dx + dz*dz < pathDist*pathDist) return false;
  }
  const trunkDist = relaxed ? 3.0 : 7.5;
  for (const tree of layout.trees) {
    const dx = x-tree.x, dz = z-tree.z;
    if (dx*dx + dz*dz < trunkDist*trunkDist) return false;
  }
  return true;
}
```
- Note `layout.pathCellList` includes all path cells. Good.
- At tree build time, layout.trees contains previously placed trees. Good.
- Need if tree canopy extends beyond island edge, some cells float. Could skip cells with hypot(wx,wz) > edgeRadius(theta) + 1? Not specified. But floating leaves beyond edge might look like canopy overhanging, okay. Could look like island edge with tree canopy. Fine. But if canopy extends far beyond, might look odd. Tree radius 17.5, canopy r5 + lobe offset 0.8r=4 + lobeR3 => max 24.5 from centre, edge radius ~22. So some canopy cells beyond island edge. That's acceptable as overhanging branches. Good.
- Need tree canopy colors 2-3 shades? We use 4 shades. Spec says pink/white in 2–3 shades, but colors list 4. Fine.
- Count maybe.

const fish = [];
const segments = [];
const scales = [0.55,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3];
for (let f=0; f<fishCount; f++) {

  const cz = layout.pond.pz + (rng()*2-1)*1;
  const maxA = Math.max(1.8, layout.pond.rx - 2.2);

  const maxB = Math.max(2.5, layout.pond.rz - 3);

}

`genUnderside(seedValue, D)`:
- Re-seed own PRNG: `const rng = mulberry32((seedValue ^ 0xC0FFEE) >>> 0);` Need same D always same result. It should use seed and D. If we use same PRNG seed for each D, but D affects loops, the sequence for layers is same for k until D. For D changes, the jitter sequence for each cell might differ because loops extend? Actually if we iterate k=1..D and x,z loops, the rng sequence for earlier layers is same regardless of D, because D only affects radius formula, not rng consumption order. But jitter per cell consumed same. So genUnderside(seed,D) deterministic. Good.
- Need edgeRadius phases same as island. We can pass edgeRadius function or reconstruct phases. Since layout.edgeRadiusFn set. But spec says genUnderside(seed, D) re-seeds its own PRNG. It can use layout.edgeRadiusFn. The same D always same result if edge phases same. Good.
- Underside array:
```
function genUnderside(seedValue, D) {
  const rng = mulberry32((seedValue ^ 0x9E3779B9) >>> 0);
  const cells = [];
  const edgeRadius = layout.edgeRadiusFn;
  for (let k=1; k<=D; k++) {
    const y = -2 - k;
    const scale = Math.pow(1 - k/(D+1), 1.3);
    for (let x=-26; x<=26; x++) {
      for (let z=-26; z<=26; z++) {
        const theta = Math.atan2(z,x);
        const jitter = (rng()*2-1)*0.8;
        const radius = edgeRadius(theta) * scale + jitter;
        if (Math.hypot(x,z) <= radius) {
          let color;
          if (k===1) {
            // first underside layer mixed with dirt
            color = rng() < 0.5 ? DIRT_COLORS[Math.floor(rng()*2)] : ROCK_COLORS[Math.floor(rng()*3)];
          } else {
            color = ROCK_COLORS[Math.floor(rng()*3)];
          }
          cells.push([x,y,z,color]);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  // stalactites
  const undersideCount = cells.length;
  const stalactiteCount = 10 + Math.floor(rng()*8); // few
  for (let s=0; s<stalactiteCount; s++) {
    const base = cells[Math.floor(rng()*undersideCount)];
    const len = 1 + Math.floor(rng()*3);

```
function buildStaticMesh() {
  if (staticMesh) remove...
  const count = world.size;
  staticMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(voxelGeometry, staticMaterial, count);
  staticMesh.castShadow = true;
  staticMesh.receiveShadow = true;
  staticMesh.frustumCulled = false;
  const fillRng = mulberry32((seed ^ 0xB5297A4D) >>> 0);
  let i=0;
  for (const voxel of world.values()) {
    const [x,y,z,color] = voxel;
    dummy.position.set(x,y,z);
    dummy.rotation.set(0,0,0);
    dummy.scale.set(1,1,1);
    dummy.updateMatrix();
    staticMesh.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
    const c = new THREE.Color(color);
    const jitter = 0.94 + fillRng()*0.12;
    c.multiplyScalar(jitter);
    staticMesh.setColorAt(i, c);
    i++;
  }
  staticMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  if (staticMesh.instanceColor) staticMesh.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
  scene.add(staticMesh);
}
```

function buildWaterMesh() {
  if (waterMesh) remove...
  const cells = layout.pond.waterCells;
  const count = cells.length;
  waterMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(voxelGeometry, waterMaterial, count);
  waterMesh.receiveShadow = true;
  waterMesh.castShadow = false;
  waterMesh.frustumCulled = false;
  waterMesh.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
  const waterRng = mulberry32((seed ^ 0x3C6EF372) >>> 0);
  for (let i=0;i<count;i++) {
    const [x,z,color] = cells[i];
    dummy.position.set(x,-0.1,z);
    dummy.rotation.set(0,0,0);
    dummy.scale.set(1,0.8,1);
    dummy.updateMatrix();
    waterMesh.setMatrixAt(i,dummy.matrix);
    tmpColor.setHex(color);
    waterMesh.setColorAt(i,tmpColor);
  }
  waterMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
  if (waterMesh.instanceColor) waterMesh.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
  waterRestPending = true;
  scene.add(waterMesh);
}
```

function buildKoiMesh() {
  if (koiMesh) remove...
  const segments = layout.koi.segments;
  const count = segments.length;
  koiMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(voxelGeometry, koiMaterial, count);

}

`rebuildWorld()`:
```
function rebuildWorld() {
  removeMeshAndDispose(staticMesh); staticMesh=null;
  removeMeshAndDispose(waterMesh); waterMesh=null;
  removeMeshAndDispose(koiMesh); koiMesh=null;
  world.clear();
  resetLayout();
  const rng = mulberry32(seed);
  buildIsland(rng);
  buildPagoda(rng);
  computePond(rng);
  buildPathAndTorii(rng);
  applyPond(rng);
  buildBridge(rng);
  buildTrees(rng);
  buildKoiData(rng);
  // auto-fit underside
  let D = 19;
  let underside = [];
  let total = 0;
  for (let iter=0; iter<14; iter++) {
    underside = genUnderside(seed, D);
    total = world.size + underside.length + layout.pond.waterCells.length + layout.koi.segments.length;
    if (total < 15600) D++;
    else if (total > 16400) D--;
    else break;
  }
  for (const [x,y,z,color] of underside) setVoxel(x,y,z,color);
  voxelCount = world.size + layout.pond.waterCells.length + layout.koi.segments.length;
  buildStaticMesh();
  buildWaterMesh();
  buildKoiMesh();
  updatePresets();
  updateStatusLine();
}
```

  underside = genUnderside(seed, D);

The recompute is not part of loop. Fine.

But to be safe, we could adjust D further until in range with a max of 14 total? Hmm. The spec explicitly says loop at most 14 times. We should follow. `rebuildPetals()`:
Need declare arrays top. Let's write:
```
let petalPos = null;
let petalRot = null;

Top.

Function:
```
function rebuildPetals() {
  if (petalMesh) { scene.remove(petalMesh); petalMesh.dispose(); petalMesh = null; }
  petalPos = new Float32Array(PETAL_CAPACITY*3);
  petalRot = new Float32Array(PETAL_CAPACITY*3);
  petalSpin = new Float32Array(PETAL_CAPACITY*3);
  petalFall = new Float32Array(PETAL_CAPACITY);
  petalSwayPhase = new Float32Array(PETAL_CAPACITY);
  petalSwayAmp = new Float32Array(PETAL_CAPACITY);
  petalRng = mulberry32((seed ^ 0x68E31DA4) >>> 0);
  const rng = petalRng;
  petalMesh = new THREE.InstancedMesh(petalGeometry, petalMaterial, PETAL_CAPACITY);
  petalMesh.frustumCulled = false;
  petalMesh.castShadow = false;
  petalMesh.receiveShadow = false;
  petalMesh.instanceMatrix.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
  for (let i=0;i<PETAL_CAPACITY;i++) {
    const i3=i*3;
    const angle = rng()*TAU;
    const radius = Math.sqrt(rng())*30;
    petalPos[i3] = Math.cos(angle)*radius;
    petalPos[i3+2] = Math.sin(angle)*radius;
    petalPos[i3+1] = -26 + rng()*70;
    petalFall[i] = 1.2 + rng()*1.4;
    petalRot[i3] = rng()*TAU;
    petalRot[i3+1] = rng()*TAU;
    petalRot[i3+2] = rng()*TAU;
    petalSpin[i3] = (rng()*2-1)*2.2;
    petalSpin[i3+1] = (rng()*2-1)*1.8;
    petalSpin[i3+2] = (rng()*2-1)*2.5;
    petalSwayPhase[i] = rng()*TAU;
    petalSwayAmp[i] = 0.4 + rng()*1.1;
    tmpColor.setHex(PETAL_COLORS[Math.floor(rng()*PETAL_COLORS.length)]);
    petalMesh.setColorAt(i, tmpColor);
  }
  petalMesh.count = Math.min(petalsTarget, PETAL_CAPACITY);
  petalMesh.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
  writePetalMatrices();
  scene.add(petalMesh);
}
```
- Note `petalMesh.instanceColor` exists after setColorAt. Good.
- Need `petalMesh.count` set before write? write writes all capacity, fine. 
- `writePetalMatrices()` uses arrays. 
- Petal material wireframe already set. Good.

`writePetalMatrices()`:
```
function writePetalMatrices() {
  if (!petalMesh || !petalPos) return;
  for (let i=0;i<PETAL_CAPACITY;i++) {
    const i3=i*3;
    dummy.position.set(petalPos[i3], petalPos[i3+1], petalPos[i3+2]);
    dummy.rotation.set(petalRot[i3], petalRot[i3+1], petalRot[i3+2]);
    dummy.scale.set(1,1,1);
    dummy.updateMatrix();
    petalMesh.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
  }
  petalMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
}
```
- Note `dummy.rotation.set` uses Euler order XYZ. Fine.

`updatePetals(dt, t)`:
```
function updatePetals(dt, t) {
  if (!petalMesh || !petalPos) return;
  const count = Math.min(petalsTarget, PETAL_CAPACITY);
  if (count <= 0) return;
  const gust = 0.65 + 0.35 * Math.sin(t * 0.55 + 0.4);
  const driftX = WIND_DIR.x * wind * 5.5 * gust;
  const driftZ = WIND_DIR.z * wind * 5.5 * gust;
  for (let i=0; i<count; i++) {
    const i3=i*3;
    petalPos[i3+1] -= petalFall[i]*dt;
    const sway = Math.sin(t*1.25 + petalSwayPhase[i]) * petalSwayAmp[i];
    petalPos[i3] += (driftX + sway*0.6) * dt;
    petalPos[i3+2] += (driftZ + Math.cos(t*1.05 + petalSwayPhase[i]) * petalSwayAmp[i] * 0.4) * dt;
    petalRot[i3] += petalSpin[i3]*dt;
    petalRot[i3+1] += petalSpin[i3+1]*dt;
    petalRot[i3+2] += petalSpin[i3+2]*dt;
    const horiz = Math.hypot(petalPos[i3], petalPos[i3+2]);
    if (petalPos[i3+1] < -26 || horiz > 46) {
      respawnPetal(i);
    }
    dummy.position.set(petalPos[i3], petalPos[i3+1], petalPos[i3+2]);
    dummy.rotation.set(petalRot[i3], petalRot[i3+1], petalRot[i3+2]);
    dummy.scale.set(1,1,1);
    dummy.updateMatrix();
    petalMesh.setMatrixAt(i, dummy.matrix);
  }
  petalMesh.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true;
}
```
- Need `respawnPetal(i)` function:
```
function respawnPetal(i) {
  const i3=i*3;
  const angle = petalRng()*TAU;
  const radius = Math.sqrt(petalRng())*30;
  petalPos[i3] = -WIND_DIR.x * wind * 18 + Math.cos(angle)*radius;
  petalPos[i3+2] = -WIND_DIR.z * wind * 18 + Math.sin(angle)*radius;
  petalPos[i3+1] = 34 + petalRng()*10;
  petalRot[i3] = petalRng()*TAU;
  petalRot[i3+1] = petalRng()*TAU;
  petalRot[i3+2] = petalRng()*TAU;
  petalFall[i] = 1.2 + petalRng()*1.4;
}
```
- Note if wind=0, no shift. Good.
- If petalsTarget=0, update returns. When slider increases, matrices for those indices may be stale? We wrote all matrices at rebuild, and update only count. If count was 0 for a while, then increased, positions for those indices haven't been simulated, but they are still initial positions. That's okay. They may be at y -26..44, some below island. Fine. If we want, on slider increase we could write matrices for newly active. They already have matrices from initial write. Good.

`rebuildFireflies()`:
```
function rebuildFireflies() {
  if (firefliesPoints) {
    scene.remove(firefliesPoints);
    if (firefliesPoints.geometry) firefliesPoints.geometry.dispose();
    firefliesPoints = null;
  }
  const rng = mulberry32((seed ^ 0x51ED270B) >>> 0);
  const positions = new Float32Array(FIREFLY_COUNT*3);
  const colors = new Float32Array(FIREFLY_COUNT*3);
  fireflyData = [];
  const baseColor = new THREE.Color(0xd9ff8a);
  for (let i=0;i<FIREFLY_COUNT;i++) {
    const angle = rng()*TAU;
    const radius = Math.sqrt(rng())*19;
    const bx = Math.cos(angle)*radius;
    const bz = Math.sin(angle)*radius;
    const by = 1.5 + rng()*9.5;
    const data = {
      bx, by, bz,
      ax1: 0.4 + rng()*1.2, ax2: 0.2 + rng()*0.8,
      ay1: 0.3 + rng()*0.9, ay2: 0.15 + rng()*0.5,
      az1: 0.4 + rng()*1.2, az2: 0.2 + rng()*0.8,
      fx1: 0.15 + rng()*0.35, fx2: 0.15 + rng()*0.35,
      fy1: 0.15 + rng()*0.35, fy2: 0.15 + rng()*0.35,
      fz1: 0.15 + rng()*0.35, fz2: 0.15 + rng()*0.35,
      px1: rng()*TAU, px2: rng()*TAU,
      py1: rng()*TAU, py2: rng()*TAU,
      pz1: rng()*TAU, pz2: rng()*TAU,
      twinkleFreq: 0.6 + rng()*1.8,
      twinklePhase: rng()*TAU,
      cr: baseColor.r, cg: baseColor.g, cb: baseColor.b
    };
    fireflyData.push(data);
    positions[i*3] = bx;
    positions[i*3+1] = by;
    positions[i*3+2] = bz;
    colors[i*3] = data.cr;
    colors[i*3+1] = data.cg;
    colors[i*3+2] = data.cb;
  }
  const geom = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  const posAttr = new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions,3);
  posAttr.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
  const colAttr = new THREE.BufferAttribute(colors,3);
  colAttr.setUsage(THREE.DynamicDrawUsage);
  geom.setAttribute('position', posAttr);
  geom.setAttribute('color', colAttr);
  firefliesPoints = new THREE.Points(geom, firefliesMaterial);
  firefliesPoints.frustumCulled = false;
  scene.add(firefliesPoints);
  updateFireflies(0);
  firefliesMaterial.opacity = nightFactor;
  firefliesPoints.visible = firefliesOn && nightFactor > 0.01;
}
```
- Need `glowTexture` set before first render. In init before rebuildFireflies: `glowTexture = makeGlowTexture(); firefliesMaterial.map = glowTexture; firefliesMaterial.needsUpdate = true;`. Good.
- Note `firefliesMaterial` has vertexColors true. Good.
- `updateFireflies(t)`:
```
function updateFireflies(t) {
  if (!firefliesPoints || !fireflyData.length) return;
  const posAttr = firefliesPoints.geometry.getAttribute('position');
  const colAttr = firefliesPoints.geometry.getAttribute('color');
  for (let i=0;i<fireflyData.length;i++) {
    const f = fireflyData[i];
    const x = f.bx + Math.sin(t*f.fx1 + f.px1)*f.ax1 + Math.sin(t*f.fx2 + f.px2)*f.ax2;
    const y = f.by + Math.sin(t*f.fy1 + f.py1)*f.ay1 + Math.sin(t*f.fy2 + f.py2)*f.ay2;
    const z = f.bz + Math.sin(t*f.fz1 + f.pz1)*f.az1 + Math.sin(t*f.fz2 + f.pz2)*f.az2;
    posAttr.setXYZ(i,x,y,z);
    const tw = 0.35 + 0.65 * (0.5 + 0.5*Math.sin(t*f.twinkleFreq + f.twinklePhase));
    colAttr.setXYZ(i, f.cr*tw, f.cg*tw, f.cb*tw);
  }
  posAttr.needsUpdate = true;
  colAttr.needsUpdate = true;
}
```
- Need `f.cr` etc linear. baseColor.r for 0xd9ff8a. Good.
- Fireflies visible only at night. Good.

`skyColorAt(h)`:
```
function skyColorAt(h) {
  let hh = ((h % 24) + 24) % 24;
  for (let i=0;i<SKY_KEYS.length-1;i++) {
    const k0=SKY_KEYS[i], k1=SKY_KEYS[i+1];
    if (hh >= k0.h && hh <= k1.h) {
      const t = (hh - k0.h) / (k1.h - k0.h);
      return skyColor.lerpColors(SKY_COLORS[i], SKY_COLORS[i+1], t);
    }
  }
  return skyColor.copy(SKY_COLORS[0]);
}
```
- At hh=0, first segment 0..4.5. Good. At hh=23, segment 21..24. Good. At hh=24 normalized 0. Good.
- Note `SKY_KEYS` last h=24. Fine.

`updatePresets()`:
```
function updatePresets() {
  presets = [];
  presets.push({ pos: new THREE.Vector3(52,40,62), target: new THREE.Vector3(0,7,0) });
  presets.push({ pos: new THREE.Vector3(29,21,42), target: new THREE.Vector3(0,16,0) });
  const pond = layout.pond;
  presets.push({ pos: new THREE.Vector3(pond.px + 15, 16, pond.pz + 14), target: new THREE.Vector3(pond.px, 0.5, pond.pz) });
  presets.push({ pos: new THREE.Vector3(8,7,41), target: new THREE.Vector3(0,6,18) });
  let farthest = null;
  let farthestDist = -1;
  for (const tree of layout.trees) {
    const d = Math.hypot(tree.x, tree.z);
    if (d > farthestDist) { farthestDist = d; farthest = tree; }
  }
  if (farthest) {
    const dir = new THREE.Vector3(farthest.x, 0, farthest.z).normalize();
    const pos = new THREE.Vector3(farthest.x + dir.x * 22, farthest.canopyY + 6, farthest.z + dir.z * 22);
    const target = new THREE.Vector3(farthest.x, farthest.canopyY, farthest.z);
    presets.push({ pos, target });
  } else {
    presets.push({ pos: new THREE.Vector3(38, 22, 40), target: new THREE.Vector3(0, 8, 0) });
  }
}
```
- Need ensure Blossoms position horizontal distance from centre >=30. farthestDist >=11, +22 =33. Good.
- Note `farthest.canopyY` = trunkHeight. Target canopy centre. Good.
- Need if no trees, fallback. Fine.

`flyToPreset(index)`:
```
function flyToPreset(index) {
  if (index < 0 || index >= presets.length) return;
  const preset = presets[index];
  flight = {
    startPos: camera.position.clone(),
    startTarget: controls.target.clone(),
    endPos: preset.pos.clone(),
    endTarget: preset.target.clone(),
    startMs: performance.now()
  };
}
```
- If controls not initialized? Buttons after init. Fine.
- `updateFlight()`:
```
function updateFlight() {
  if (!flight) return;
  const k = clamp((performance.now() - flight.startMs) / 1200, 0, 1);
  const e = easeInOutCubic(k);
  camera.position.lerpVectors(flight.startPos, flight.endPos, e);
  controls.target.lerpVectors(flight.startTarget, flight.endTarget, e);
  if (k >= 1) flight = null;
}
```
- Note `lerpVectors` on camera.position and controls.target. Good.
- Flight progress wall clock. Good.
- Do not disable controls. Good.

`init()`:
```
function init() {
  scene = new THREE.Scene();
  scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xa8d8f8);
  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(0xa8d8f8, 130, 420);

  camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth/window.innerHeight, 0.5, 900);
  camera.position.set(52,40,62);

  renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({antialias:true});
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
  renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
  renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
  document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

  controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
  controls.enableDamping = true;
  controls.dampingFactor = 0.08;
  controls.minDistance = 10;
  controls.maxDistance = 240;
  controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.6;
  controls.autoRotate = autoRotate;
  controls.target.set(0,7,0);
  controls.update();
  controls.addEventListener('start', () => { flight = null; });

  sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff6e8, 3.0);
  sun.castShadow = true;
  sun.shadow.mapSize.set(2048,2048);
  sun.shadow.camera.left = -46;
  sun.shadow.camera.right = 46;
  sun.shadow.camera.top = 46;
  sun.shadow.camera.bottom = -46;
  sun.shadow.camera.near = 1;
  sun.shadow.camera.far = 240;
  sun.shadow.bias = -0.0005;
  sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.05;
  sun.target.position.set(0,0,0);
  scene.add(sun);
  scene.add(sun.target);

  hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xa8d8f8, 0x6b5a45, 1.5);
  scene.add(hemi);

  moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x9fb8ff, 0);
  moon.position.copy(moonDir).multiplyScalar(80);
  moon.target.position.set(0,0,0);
  scene.add(moon);
  scene.add(moon.target);

  glowTexture = makeGlowTexture();
  firefliesMaterial.map = glowTexture;
  firefliesMaterial.needsUpdate = true;

  setupUI();
  rebuildWorld();
  rebuildPetals();
  rebuildFireflies();
  applyTime(hours);
  syncClockUI();
  updateStatusLine();

  lastFrameMs = performance.now();
  fpsLastMs = lastFrameMs;
  fpsFrameCount = 0;
  animate();
}
```

`animate()`:
```
function animate() {
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
  const now = performance.now();
  let dt = (now - lastFrameMs) / 1000;
  if (!isFinite(dt) || dt < 0) dt = 0;
  dt = Math.min(dt, 0.1);
  lastFrameMs = now;
  elapsedSeconds += dt;

  if (playing) {
    hours += dt * cycleSpeed;
    if (hours >= 24) hours -= 24;
    if (hours < 0) hours += 24;
    syncClockUI();
  }

  applyTime(hours);
  animateWater(elapsedSeconds);
  writeKoiMatrices(elapsedSeconds);
  updatePetals(dt, elapsedSeconds);
  updateFireflies(elapsedSeconds);
  updateFlight();
  controls.update();
  renderer.render(scene, camera);

  fpsFrameCount++;
  if (now - fpsLastMs >= 1000) {
    const elapsed = (now - fpsLastMs) / 1000;
    fps = Math.round(fpsFrameCount / elapsed);
    fpsFrameCount = 0;
    fpsLastMs = now;
    updateStatusLine();
  }
}
```

Potential issue: Koi segment yaw along path tangent. We compute tangent. Good.
- Segment j trails by phase delay. We use `delay = j*0.22` and `phase = phase0 + dir*omega*t - dir*delay`. Good.
- If dir=-1, trail means phase + delay? Let's verify: Motion phase derivative = dir*omega. At time t, head phase = phase0 + dir*omega*t. A trailing segment should be at the position the head was at earlier time t - Δt, so phase = phase0 + dir*omega*(t - Δt) = head phase - dir*omega*Δt. Since omega*Δt ~ delay, phase = head phase - dir*delay. Our formula uses `- dir*delay`. Correct. Good.
- Tangent: derivative of (a cos phase, b sin phase) w.r.t time = (-a sin phase * dir*omega, b cos phase * dir*omega). We use tx = -a sin phase * dir, tz = b cos phase * dir. Yaw = atan2(tx,tz). Good.
- Segment scales. Good.
- One white segment per fish. Good.

Potential issue: `mulberry32` uses `a |= 0` and `Math.imul`. Good.
- Standard implementation:
```
function mulberry32(a) {
  return function() {
    a |= 0;
    a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) | 0;
    let t = Math.imul(a ^ (a >>> 15), 1 | a);
    t = (t + Math.imul(t ^ (t >>> 7), 61 | t)) ^ t;
    return ((t ^ (t >>> 14)) >>> 0) / 4294967296;
  };
}
```
Need parentheses. Good.
- This returns deterministic floats. Good.

Tree placement and canopy:
- `isTreeSpotValid` uses `layout.pathCellList`. But `layout.pathCellList` includes path cells x -1..1 z9..20. It is populated before trees. Good.

- `isTreeSpotValid` checks az. For x=0,z=-15, atan2(0,-15)=π -> 180°, allowed. Good.
- For x=-15,z=0, atan2(-15,0)=-π/2 -> -90°, add 360 ->270°, allowed. Good.
- For x=15,z=0, 90°, not allowed. Good.
- For x=0,z=15, 0°, not allowed. Good.
- So trees only in back-left arc. Good.
- Radius 11-17.5. Good.

Tree canopy bounds:

Potential issue: `smoothstep` function:
```
function smoothstep(edge0, edge1, x) {
  const t = clamp((x - edge0) / (edge1 - edge0), 0, 1);
  return t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
}
```
Good.

Potential issue: `clamp`:
```
function clamp(v, min, max) { return Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v)); }
```
Good.

Potential issue: `lerp`:
```
function lerp(a,b,t) { return a + (b-a)*t; }
```
Good.

Potential issue: `easeInOutCubic`:
```
function easeInOutCubic(t) {
  return t < 0.5 ? 4*t*t*t : 1 - Math.pow(-2*t + 2, 3) / 2;
}
```
Good.

Potential issue: `formatTime`:
```
function formatTime(h) {
  const normalized = ((h % 24) + 24) % 24;
  const totalMinutes = Math.floor(normalized * 60 + 0.000001);
  const hh = Math.floor(totalMinutes / 60) % 24;
  const mm = totalMinutes % 60;
  return String(hh).padStart(2, '0') + ':' + String(mm).padStart(2, '0');
}
```
Good.

Potential issue: `padStart` supported. Good.

Potential issue: `Math.floor(normalized*60 + 0.000001)` for 10.5 -> 630.000001 ->630. Good.

Potential issue: `skyColorAt`:
```
function skyColorAt(h) {
  let hh = ((h % 24) + 24) % 24;
  for (let i = 0; i < SKY_KEYS.length - 1; i++) {
    const k0 = SKY_KEYS[i];
    const k1 = SKY_KEYS[i + 1];
    if (hh >= k0.h && hh <= k1.h) {
      const t = (hh - k0.h) / (k1.h - k0.h);
      return skyColor.lerpColors(SKY_COLORS[i], SKY_COLORS[i + 1], t);
    }
  }
  return skyColor.copy(SKY_COLORS[0]);
}
```
Good.
- At hh=4.5, first segment k0=0,k1=4.5, t=1 -> color 4.5. Good.
- At hh=4.5001, next segment k0=4.5,k1=6. Good.
- At hh=21, segment 19.5..21 t=1 -> 21. Good.
- At hh=23.9, segment 21..24 t=0.966. Good.
- At hh=24 normalized 0 -> first segment. Good.

Potential issue: `SKY_KEYS` includes 24. Good.

Potential issue: `SKY_COLORS` maps. Good.

Potential issue: `applyTime`:
```
function applyTime(h) {
  const a = (h - 6) / 24 * TAU;
  const sinA = Math.sin(a);
  sunDir.set(0.8 * Math.cos(a), sinA, 0.45).normalize();
  sun.position.copy(sunDir).multiplyScalar(90);
  sun.target.position.set(0, 0, 0);
  const d = smoothstep(-0.1, 0.25, sinA);
  sun.intensity = 3.0 * d;
  const warmMix = smoothstep(-0.05, 0.65, sinA);
  sun.color.lerpColors(sunWarmColor, sunWhiteColor, warmMix);
  moon.position.copy(moonDir).multiplyScalar(80);
  moon.target.position.set(0, 0, 0);
  moon.intensity = 0.8 * (1 - d);
  hemi.intensity = lerp(0.45, 1.5, d);
  const sky = skyColorAt(h);
  scene.background.copy(sky);
  scene.fog.color.copy(sky);
  hemi.color.copy(sky);
  hemi.groundColor.copy(groundColor);
  nightFactor = h >= 12 ? smoothstep(18.5, 20, h) : 1 - smoothstep(4.5, 6, h);
  firefliesMaterial.opacity = nightFactor;
  if (firefliesPoints) {
    firefliesPoints.visible = firefliesOn && nightFactor > 0.01;
  }
  sun.castShadow = shadowsOn;
}
```
- `h` could be 24. `h>=12` true. nightFactor = smoothstep(18.5,20,24)=1. Good.
- `h` could be 0. `h>=12` false. nightFactor = 1 - smoothstep(4.5,6,0)=1. Good.
- `h` could be 10.5. h<12, nightFactor=1-smoothstep(4.5,6,10.5)=1-1=0. Good.
- `h` could be 12. h>=12, smoothstep(18.5,20,12)=0. Good.
- `h` could be 4.5. h<12, 1-smoothstep(4.5,6,4.5)=1-0=1. Good.
- `h` could be 6. 1-smoothstep(4.5,6,6)=0. Good.
- Good.

Potential issue: `updatePresets`:
```
function updatePresets() {
  presets = [
    { pos: new THREE.Vector3(52, 40, 62), target: new THREE.Vector3(0, 7, 0) },
    { pos: new THREE.Vector3(29, 21, 42), target: new THREE.Vector3(0, 16, 0) },
    { pos: new THREE.Vector3(layout.pond.px + 15, 16, layout.pond.pz + 14), target: new THREE.Vector3(layout.pond.px, 0.5, layout.pond.pz) },
    { pos: new THREE.Vector3(8, 7, 41), target: new THREE.Vector3(0, 6, 18) }
  ];
  let farthest = null;
  let farthestDist = -1;
  for (const tree of layout.trees) {
    const dist = Math.hypot(tree.x, tree.z);
    if (dist > farthestDist) {
      farthestDist = dist;
      farthest = tree;
    }
  }
  if (farthest) {
    const dir = new THREE.Vector3(farthest.x, 0, farthest.z).normalize();
    const pos = new THREE.Vector3(farthest.x + dir.x * 22, farthest.canopyY + 6, farthest.z + dir.z * 22);
    const target = new THREE.Vector3(farthest.x, farthest.canopyY, farthest.z);
    presets.push({ pos, target });
  } else {
    presets.push({ pos: new THREE.Vector3(38, 22, 40), target: new THREE.Vector3(0, 8, 0) });
  }
}
```
- Good.
- If `farthest.x` and `farthest.z` both 0? Not possible. Good.
- `dir` normalized. Good.
- `pos` horizontal distance from centre = |tree| + 22 >=33. Good.
- `target` canopy centre. Good.

Potential issue: `flyToPreset`:
```
function flyToPreset(index) {
  if (index < 0 || index >= presets.length) return;
  const preset = presets[index];
  flight = {
    startPos: camera.position.clone(),
    startTarget: controls.target.clone(),
    endPos: preset.pos.clone(),
    endTarget: preset.target.clone(),
    startMs: performance.now()
  };
}
```
- Good.

Potential issue: `updateFlight`:
```
function updateFlight() {
  if (!flight) return;
  const k = clamp((performance.now() - flight.startMs) / 1200, 0, 1);
  const e = easeInOutCubic(k);
  camera.position.lerpVectors(flight.startPos, flight.endPos, e);
  controls.target.lerpVectors(flight.startTarget, flight.endTarget, e);
  if (k >= 1) {
    flight = null;
  }
}
```
- Good.

Potential issue: `init`:
```
function init() {
  scene = new THREE.Scene();
  scene.background = new THREE.Color(0xa8d8f8);
  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(0xa8d8f8, 130, 420);

  camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(45, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.5, 900);
  camera.position.set(52, 40, 62);

  renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio, 2));
  renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight);
  renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true;
  renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
  document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement);

  controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
  controls.enableDamping = true;
  controls.dampingFactor = 0.08;
  controls.minDistance = 10;
  controls.maxDistance = 240;
  controls.autoRotateSpeed = 0.6;
  controls.autoRotate = autoRotate;
  controls.target.set(0, 7, 0);
  controls.update();
  controls.addEventListener('start', () => {
    flight = null;
  });

  sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xfff6e8, 3.0);
  sun.castShadow = true;
  sun.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048);
  sun.shadow.camera.left = -46;
  sun.shadow.camera.right = 46;
  sun.shadow.camera.top = 46;
  sun.shadow.camera.bottom = -46;
  sun.shadow.camera.near = 1;
  sun.shadow.camera.far = 240;
  sun.shadow.bias = -0.0005;
  sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.05;
  sun.shadow.camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  sun.target.position.set(0, 0, 0);
  scene.add(sun);
  scene.add(sun.target);

  hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xa8d8f8, 0x6b5a45, 1.5);
  scene.add(hemi);

  moon = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x9fb8ff, 0);
  moon.position.copy(moonDir).multiplyScalar(80);
  moon.target.position.set(0, 0, 0);
  scene.add(moon);
  scene.add(moon.target);

  glowTexture = makeGlowTexture();
  firefliesMaterial.map = glowTexture;
  firefliesMaterial.needsUpdate = true;

  setupUI();
  rebuildWorld();
  rebuildPetals();
  rebuildFireflies();
  applyTime(hours);
  syncClockUI();
  updateStatusLine();

  lastFrameMs = performance.now();
  fpsLastMs = lastFrameMs;
  fpsFrameCount = 0;
  animate();
}
```
